# Persistent damage spells — proposed video game rules

**Pending exact authored acceptance.** Twenty existing spell identities retain their original access. The full source-specific procedures below include the actual conference corrections. Runtime and balance remain separate.

## Shared procedures

### P20-CASTING-01

Reuse actual class-access/repertoire, paid costs, component, concentration and Dissonance/Refrain procedures from the exact registry. None of these drafts grants a new class row, rank, upcast or resource maximum. A continuous field, stored rider or command does not itself cast again. Actual target/caster predicates are rechecked at the source-defined checkpoint, without refund or diagnostic disclosure from hidden failure.

### P20-DIGITAL-GEOMETRY-01

NEW bounded clarification for these 20 proposals: measure the actual occupied body against the continuous authored volume/surface, with actual cover/line of effect. Five-foot lengths remain real lengths, not a rounding of positions onto tabletop squares. Source-authored five-foot destructible/fuel partitions remain fixed object identities; changing path sampling, zoom or visual mesh subdivision cannot create more targets, HP or exposure. Evaluate a genuine entry at the first boundary crossing/contact, resolve it once before continuing the same trajectory, and preserve actual remaining movement. Teleport crosses no intermediate path; an arrival can trigger only a source explicitly authorizing arrival. Snapshot simultaneous recipients once before their consequences; destroyed cover creates no second wave.

### P20-CLOCKS-01

NEW explicit cohort bindings: absolute field deadlines are resolution+60 seconds where the procedure states one minute; expiry wins at equality. Source-owned pulses at resolution and +6 through +54 do not require a caster turn or grant a creature turn. A finite target/source marker schedule records its original regular markers at creation under accepted time ordering; reorder, extra turns and departure preserve the original virtual schedule, six seconds apart. Explicit future-turn endpoints exclude an in-progress turn/phase; an ending save expressly including current END keeps that exception. At START, apply accepted expiry/recovery/death-save ordering before periodic damage. At END, due damage/eligible ending saves precede that source endpoint cleanup, except a card explicitly expires before its redundant last save. Suppression consumes a due scheduled opportunity with no damage or catch-up. Ended creature identities have no recipient; administrative deadline bookkeeping never resurrects one.

### P20-ATTACHED-PHASE-01

NEW source-specific bridge for the target-clock proposals in this cohort, not a general companion turn: an owner-attached recipient uses the original owner regular attached-phase beginning/end only for the stated spell packet or ending save. A future phase excludes one already underway. If no such real phase exists, retain a six-second spell-only checkpoint and finite original endpoint. This checkpoint supplies only a due packet/save or expiry that the spell actually grants; it supplies no Action, movement, Bonus Action, Reaction refresh, recharge, or ordinary death save. Source-required real Action/command maintenance cannot execute at an administrative checkpoint. Root identity, prior spent exposures, endpoint and finite tick count survive controller or schedule changes.

### P20-FAMILY-EXPOSURE-01

NEW per-source anti-duplication applies only where the individual procedure names a family cap: record target identity, named spell family, explicit category and numbered round. Different owners/casts share that named ledger; distinct spell names and explicitly separate Wall of Fire categories do not merge. Outside initiative use the scene continuing six-second exposure cycle, fixed before the relevant exposure. Preserve its spent state through a transition to the current combat round until that mapped interval ends; never refresh just by changing timing mode. Immunity/save/prevention after actual admission still consumes the exposure; wholly suppressed or geometrically ineligible sources cannot claim it. Simultaneous eligible sources use established application order then stable identity. Immolation instead uses the already accepted ongoing-fire arbitration at each identical target marker.

### P20-DAMAGE-FUNDING-01

Reuse actual typed defenses, temporary HP, HP floors, Wounds, concentration, death/Null life order and one original recipient event. No inferred damage amount from structural failure or overkill. ActualDamageDealt remains real tempHP absorption plus normal HP loss. Healing/funding is a separate consumer: first exclude any terminal branch whose actual transfer/source flag forbids derived healing or recovery, then apply the spell-specific donor predicate, sum eligible branches once and floor the half once. Never count a split parent again. Burden Shared and Life Linked restrictions remain operative; a numeric branch amount alone is not funding permission. Preserve the distinct pending Leeching Plague donor exclusions and Enervation wider proposal for explicit Director disposition.

### P20-RNG-INFORMATION-01

Authored dice are engine RNG. Original component identity, draw method, save factor and cached ordinal value remain private engine state unless an actual separately accepted presentation policy grants the exact disclosure. This candidate grants no new public dice viewer, hidden-target list, successful-save feedback, enemy-identity scan or Fate eligibility. Shared damage dice do not merge recipient roots. The accepted exact DE4 five-entry presentation policy applies only where its actual source predicates pass; it does not automatically disclose any private periodic, delayed, penalty or random-selection operation in this cohort. An actual qualifying OVERT-ATTACK d20 can use that entry without exposing a hidden Synaptic Static d6, hidden modifier cause or later damage pool. No sixth presentation entry is proposed here. No RNG seed or future draw is exposed. If a source has no admitted recipient and no explicit source-authored event-generation rule, do not invent a damage event merely to publish a roll.

### P20-ROOT-01

Every damage source uses its explicit pending per-source mapping in damageRootProposals. These are NEW decisions requiring concurrence; accepted Forbiddance/Chain Arc/Skybrand and lower nightmare rules do not silently generalize. Root creation alone spends no Action, deals no damage, triggers no Wound, generates no material, and grants no hidden information. Pure transfer retains flags; an attached component can retain an old flag while a genuinely new operated attack creates its own root. Physical fire and Sonic debris mappings are bounded to the exact finite proposed outputs.

### P20-SUPPRESSION-01

Reuse the exact actual suppressor/removal source. Lesser Antimagic, rank limits and source versus expression tests keep their accepted scope. AC6 Globe is not general antimagic: its fixed boundary blocks only its actual incoming rank-0–5 source cases, does not ban all internal casting, and does not erase an already attached buff merely because a creature enters. A fixed external ongoing field and a target-attached rider have different origin facts. A spell object is not a creature; physical Inferno fuel/rubble after actual creation is not a sustained magical field. No suppression pauses a deadline or restores a destroyed body, cleared crystal patch, spent shot or old command.

## Inferno Wave

| Class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 4 | 11 | 7 |
| Dragoon | Evocation | 4 | 18 | 7 |
| Mage | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |

Action; V/S/F; instantaneous wave, no concentration. KEEP 60-foot line and 6d6 fire. NEW: a straight line from the caster, five feet wide and five feet high, in a declared direction; being its origin alone does not include the caster. Stop at a total solid obstruction. Snapshot all eligible creatures, each making DEX for full 6d6 fire on failure or half on success, with one shared raw roll. The wave itself has no general object-damage packet. KEEP its distinct ground-ignition clause: after the snapshot, ignite genuinely exposed combustible ground/floor material intersecting this footprint, including dry surface vegetation or an actually burnable wooden surface. Bare stone, water, saturated ground and unsupported air supply no fuel. NEW finite small-fire completion: divide actual ignited footprint into at most twelve five-foot patches, each clipped to the line and its available fuel. Before applying a patch, record the real combustible substrate, the finite amount that actually ignites, its physical burn endpoint no later than sixty seconds after ignition, and actual extinguishing method. If available fuel burns out sooner, use that earlier endpoint; no invented fuel mass, structural HP loss or total floor collapse follows from the spell. Each patch is an ordinary physical flame, not a continuing spell or Core Burning applied to a creature. It remains until fuel/endpoint or real extinguishing, independently of caster concentration, dispel or suppression of other magic. NEW hazard: a creature in contact with its burning surface takes 1d6 fire without a save on first actual entry or its regular START there, at most once per Inferno-ground-fire family/target/round across all patches and owners. A creature eligible for the initial wave counts as exposed to this family for that same round even if its save/defenses prevent damage; it takes no second immediate ground packet. No creation pulse, airborne heat damage or extra-turn START is added. A wholly suppressed original cast creates neither wave nor ignition; suppression after actual ignition cannot unburn material. Real dousing or complete smothering can end a contacted patch; one adjacent creature may spend an Action using actual sufficient water, sand or a usable nonburning cover to extinguish one patch, with no roll and no fabricated equipment. A source effect such as Tidal Wave can extinguish the ordinary flames it genuinely contacts. NEW conservative fire boundary: these patches do not automatically spread or persist past their recorded endpoint. Continued/spreading environmental fire requires a separately established ordinary hazard and remaining real fuel, never a fresh spell packet or free manufactured stock. Reignition is possible only for actual remaining unburned material and never resets a current round exposure.

##### name

finite ordinary ignited fuel

##### rootKind

one ignition/patch provenance record; recipient-specific damage roots descend from it

##### dealer

actual original caster character; no creature identity is created for a field, beam or object cannon

##### creationCheckpoint

At successful ignition of each admitted finite patch, store actual igniting caster and then-current Null flag. On that patch damaging a later recipient, allocate recipient-specific descendants from that stored provenance.

##### descendants

The same finite patch keeps its origin flag through its short printed life. This proposal grants no spreading fire, fuel, global ownership, or provenance for an independently ignited/spread hazard.

#### reason

The immediate wave and physical ignition are distinct original outputs. Preserve causal origin without reclassifying mundane fire as a maintained spell. Physical propagation remains a separately unresolved environment decision.

## Freezing Rain

| Class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 4 | 11 | 7 |
| Dragoon | Evocation | 4 | 18 | 7 |
| Mage | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |

Action; V/S/F. KEEP the casting-point range of 30 feet separately from the 30-foot radius and the 4d6 cold per round. NEW replace the contradictory Instantaneous duration with concentration up to sixty elapsed seconds. Choose a perceived point within range with line of effect; create a fixed vertical cylinder thirty feet in radius and twenty feet high, centered vertically on that point. Its volume and affected surfaces are clipped by actual solid cover from the center; rain does not cross a roof or sealed floor by name alone. All legal creatures in that volume make CON at creation, then at each source six-second pulse before expiry. Roll shared 4d6 cold for each pulse: full on failure, half on success. NEW family cap: initial and periodic cold together affect a target at most once per Freezing Rain identity per round across owners/casts. Each pulse takes a fresh geometry/cover snapshot. Entry, forced movement, reentry, extra turns or suppression ending adds no cold tick; entering can expose a creature at the next genuine pulse. Damage is independent of the slippery ground. NEW slippery surface: actual exposed walkable surfaces inside the active cylinder cost double ground movement, using ordinary difficult-terrain arbitration without multiplying an existing double cost. At the first voluntary ground-movement expenditure on a covered surface in a round, after the first up-to-five-feet segment, make DEX against the first encountered active rain’s recorded DC; failure applies Prone if the creature can receive it. This slip check is family once/target/round, shared across casts, walking and attempts to stand then move. Already Prone creatures spend the same opportunity but receive no additional condition; ordinary crawling and standing costs remain. Standing alone does not trigger it, and forced movement, falling, flight and teleportation do not themselves spend ground Speed or force a slip save. A failed creature still chooses how to use remaining legal movement/actions. The rain supplies no Frozen, restrained body, attack penalty or magical Speed reduction; terrain therefore is not a Balanced Elements slow. Suppression removes only that source’s active cold/terrain effects while original clocks and spent history continue. At ending, all its magical rain and slipperiness vanish, with no residual ice, drinking water, repair material or harvestable stock. Existing mundane weather and unrelated terrain remain.

CURRENT MOVEMENT PRECISION — NEW P20-RAIN-CONTACT-07, Freezing Rain only: For the first continuous voluntary ground trajectory contacting an operative covered surface in a numbered round, accumulate actual contacted distance, not nominal Speed expenditure. Resolve the one DEX slip check at the earliest of (a) five actual contacted feet, (b) a genuine earlier voluntary stop, or (c) the boundary at which that continuous contacted portion ends because the continuing voluntary trajectory leaves all operative Freezing Rain-covered surface. Under (c), resolve the check after paying the contacted distance and before spending movement on the outside continuation. A zero-duration frame/input split is not a stop or a coverage exit; touching or crossing internal patch/owner boundaries without leaving the union of operative covered surface does not reset the accumulator. At a geometric contact-loss boundary use the completed contacted path length up to that boundary; no epsilon distance grants a second check or free travel. A one-foot real move followed by a stop checks after that foot. A short exit without stopping likewise checks before outside continuation; it cannot evade the check solely by leaving before five feet. If movement remains wholly within covered surface and has not stopped, retain the accumulated length across frame and command subdivisions until one of the stated checkpoints occurs. Standing alone, teleportation, airborne motion, Falling and forced movement still do not themselves generate this voluntary ground-slip check. A failed check applies actual Prone without refund; continuation requires the creature's then-legal crawling, standing and movement budgets. The family once-per-target/per-round opportunity is shared across owners and remains spent even when Prone immunity prevents the consequence. This is not a global slip rule or a Crystalline Growth timing change.

## Ice Storm

| Class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 4 | 11 | 7 |
| Dragoon | Evocation | 4 | 18 | 7 |
| Mage | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |

Action; range 60 feet; V/S/F. KEEP immediate 4d6 bludgeoning PLUS 4d6 cold and difficult terrain. NEW choose a perceived supported surface point with line of effect; create a fixed cylinder of twenty-foot radius and forty-foot height upward from that point, clipped by actual obstruction from the origin. The immediate hail affects all creatures in that region; no general object/roof destruction. Each makes one DEX save. Roll one shared 4d6 bludgeoning and one shared 4d6 cold; failure receives both full typed subamounts, success floors half of each separately. Apply type-specific defenses and combine the surviving typed components as one simultaneous damage event per original recipient. This is not two saves, two hits or two automatically duplicated Wound/concentration triggers; actual damage-sharing preserves type and the one original-recipient root. NEW residual terrain completion: after the instantaneous hail, retain a magical difficult-terrain footprint on genuinely exposed walkable surfaces within the affected cylinder for sixty elapsed seconds, without concentration. The source’s Instantaneous cast metadata is preserved as history; this expressly created rank-4 residual is ongoing and can be removed/suppressed by an actually qualifying effect. Ground movement cost doubles under normal terrain arbitration; overlapping same-cost terrain never quadruples it. There is no continuing hail damage, slip save, Prone, Frozen, cold vulnerability, obscuration or structural support. A source’s suppressed region has no terrain cost while its deadline runs. Clearing ordinary debris does not remove this magical field; actual dispel/removal can end it within its real area/source scope. At expiry or removal its transient hail/ice leaves no inventory or useful water, while independent physical changes already caused by legitimate other events remain. NEW one residual footprint per caster from this spell: a later successful resolution replaces that caster’s prior residual only, and never reverses earlier hail damage; a countered cast leaves the old nonconcentration footprint. Different casters retain separate fields and clocks.

NEW P20-ICE-MIXED-07 — Ice Storm only. Preserve one simultaneous original-recipient damage event with bludgeoning and cold subamounts. Roll each printed 4d6 pool once for the cast. Apply the stated save factor to each type and floor each half separately. The following distribution rule grants no defense and changes no defense's eligibility, required activation, actual flat amount, frequency or explicitly stated before/after-resistance stage.

For competing flat damage reductions governed by CLASS_PLAYTEST_RULES.md, select only the strongest applicable flat amount for this event, not one winner at each stage. An explicit after-resistance clause changes the selected source's stage; it does not independently authorize stacking with another competing class flat reduction. If equal amounts from different sources remain tied and no exact accepted priority decides them, NEW Ice-Storm-only tie completion: use ascending immutable source-card ID, then original owner ID and application ID. Freeze that selection before allocation and do not optimize it using later resistance, temporary HP, Wounds, or the player's preferred outcome. Independent barriers, explicit reductions outside that strongest-class-DR arbitration and real sharing operations retain their separate source permissions and stages; this rule neither creates them nor deletes them.

At the selected source's actual reduction stage, aggregate all currently eligible amounts of the same canonical damage type. Let B=min(the selected source's flat reduction, the sum of its eligible nonnegative typed amounts). Allocate B in exact rational proportion to those amounts. Give each type the floor of its share, then assign remaining integer points to the largest fractional remainders, with exact ties in ascending canonical damage-type ID. A zero amount receives no allocation. Splitting one type into dice or rows cannot change the result. Do not reduce any component below zero, apply a source's flat amount once per type, or select an allocation using later immunity/resistance. Use exact rationals or equivalent integer arithmetic, not floating-point tie guesses.

An ordinary selected class reduction resolves before resistance/immunity; then apply each actual typed defense and round under its source. A selected Spinal Plating or other exact after-resistance reduction acts on the amounts remaining after that stage instead. Retain surviving typed lineage through any actual later barrier, sharing or transfer. Resolve one grouped original-recipient temporary-HP/HP/Wound/zero event. Transfer recipients retain only their own actual lifecycle consequences and source flags. There is no extra cast, attack, save, Wound trigger, copied payment or fresh Null root merely because this packet contains two types. This rule is separately proposed for Ice Storm; the pending Meteor option is historical comparison, not its authority, and no other mixed-damage spell inherits it.

## Wall of Fire

| Class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 4 | 11 | 7 |
| Dragoon | Evocation | 4 | 18 | 7 |
| Mage | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |

Action; range 60 feet; V/S/F; KEEP concentration up to one minute, using the absolute sixty-second deadline. NEW create one stationary straight vertical wall up to sixty feet long, twenty feet high and one foot thick. Declare its orientation and endpoints; every point of the wall must be within sixty feet of the caster at placement and supported by caster line of effect, otherwise shorten or choose a legal placement before commitment. Actual solid barriers clip it; no curved enclosure or wall movement is granted. It is nonphysical magical flame: no solid cover, opaque sight block, imposed movement cost or automatic ignition. KEEP two distinct damage clauses with explicit triggers. Nearby means a creature’s occupied space is within ten feet of any wall volume on either side, including within it, with line of effect from a nearest relevant flame point. At creation snapshot nearby creatures; they make DEX for one shared 5d8 fire, half on success. Thereafter nearby damage is checked at affected regular END/attached-phase END under the shared bridge. Passing through means an actual trajectory or arriving body first enters the wall volume from outside; voluntary and forced entry qualify, and teleporting into it qualifies as arrival, but teleporting across without occupying it does not. Each genuine eligible crossing uses DEX for 5d8 fire, half on success, and does not require a caster action. NEW separate family caps across all Wall of Fire instances: at most one crossing packet AND at most one nearby packet per target per numbered round. Creation spends only the nearby category, never invents a crossing; leaving/reentering or a second wall cannot reset a spent category. Thus a creature can take two real packets in a round if it both passes through and qualifies for nearby heat, but staying within the wall grants no extra crossing. The packets remain separate events with separate actual defenses/hooks. One simultaneous entry event affecting several bodies uses one shared roll for those bodies; genuinely separate entry/end events roll independently. No attack critical or Burning component is added. Ongoing flame cannot apply a new trigger while suppressed; missed END checks are not queued and resumption is not recreation or movement of the wall. Expiry/concentration loss/removal ends future effects, with no physical fuel, lingering fire or stored heat. Independently active other walls retain their own source records, but the family category history remains shared. A creation pulse finishes its already snapshotted recipients even if that pulse ends concentration; no later crossing or END pulse then occurs.

## Vitriolic Sphere

| Class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 4 | 11 | 7 |
| Dragoon | Evocation | 4 | 18 | 7 |
| Mage | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |

Action; range 60 feet; V/S/F. KEEP 10d4 acid initially and 5d4 next round. NEW choose a perceived point with line of effect and affect all creatures in a twenty-foot-radius sphere with center line of effect; no object or carried-inventory damage. At the initial snapshot each creature makes one DEX save; roll 10d4 once, full on failure and half on success. NEW both save outcomes can carry the delayed source clause: after initial consequences, attach one pending rank-4 magical acid rider to each same continuing eligible creature, recording that initial save’s full-or-half factor, unless an actual defense prevents attachment. Initial zero damage alone does not prevent it. Actual death/ended identity prevents attachment; zero HP alone need not. At the caster’s first future original regular START, excluding a START already underway, roll one shared 5d4 for that cast’s surviving due recipients; each uses its stored initial save factor, flooring half then applying its current acid defenses. Outside initiative the delayed marker is application +6 seconds. Preserve that originally chosen virtual marker or absolute deadline through reorder, initiative changes, absence and extra turns. NEW this finite ongoing rider expressly survives the original Instantaneous cast metadata, needs no concentration, and can be ended by an actually qualifying rank/source removal before its marker. A rider suppressed at the marker consumes its single opportunity without damage and ends; no later replay. Moving, leaving the sphere or losing caster sight/range does not end an already established rider. Actual death, destruction/ended creature identity or replacement ends it; a corpse, resummon or later resurrection does not inherit it. Distinct paid casts coexist with independent riders rather than erasing each other; simultaneous due casts resolve in stable application order, each sharing only its own raw delayed roll. The delayed event is neither a cast nor attack: no new costs, cadence, Counterspell window or critical dice. Ordinary damage hooks retain their actual causal source and caps. NEW there is no generic wash/scrape removal or extractable acid. No new entrant, old location, extra turn, remnant object or previously ended recipient receives a delayed packet. No second save, third tick or multiplication by other targets is granted.

CURRENT DELAYED-POOL PRECISION: At this cast’s single original future caster START or fixed outside-initiative deadline, snapshot the same still-valid due attached recipients and their actual suppression. If at least one has an operative due packet, generate one private 5d4 pool for this cast and apply each recipient’s stored save factor and current defenses. A suppressed recipient consumes its one opportunity without receiving the pool; if no recipient has an operative packet, no damage pool is needed. No public output distinguishes an empty, hidden, suppressed or destroyed recipient list. Caster death does not reset or cancel this nonconcentration rider; ended recipient identity does. These are NEW source-specific engine completion choices, not a global empty-area rule.

## Storm Sphere

| Class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 4 | 11 | 7 |
| Dragoon | Evocation | 4 | 18 | 7 |
| Mage | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |

Action; range 60 feet; V/S/F. KEEP 4d6 bludgeoning in an area and 4d6 lightning to a nearby target each round. NEW resolve the contradictory Instantaneous label as one fixed twenty-foot-radius sphere with concentration up to sixty elapsed seconds. Choose a perceived center within range with line of effect; actual origin barriers clip each pulse. At creation and each source six-second pulse before expiry, all creatures presently inside with center line of effect make STR: failure receives a shared 4d6 bludgeoning, success half. NEW initial/periodic bludgeoning is family once per target per round across all Storm Sphere owners/casts. Entry, reentry, forced movement, extra turns and suppression ending are not additional bludgeoning triggers. There is no forced movement, difficult terrain, obscuration or object packet. NEW lightning control: while this sphere is active, on your own regular turn you may spend an available Bonus Action to fire one bolt at one creature you presently perceive, within sixty feet of the sphere center and with center-to-target line of effect. This includes the casting turn if a Bonus Action remains; a pulse never supplies that Bonus Action. Target and source are committed before the attack, with no post-roll substitution. Make one ranged spell attack using your actual modifier against target AC, applying cover from the sphere center and the ordinary attacker’s restrictions; a hostile creature within five feet of you still imposes the normal close-quarters ranged-attack rule when it qualifies. Merely being inside or next to the sphere grants no advantage. Hit deals 4d6 lightning; natural attack critical doubles only these bolt dice to 8d6. Miss deals none. The bolt is a consequence of the existing spell, not a new cast/payment/cadence event. NEW at most one bolt per source character per numbered round from this identity across casts; an extra turn, command transfer, recast or second Bonus Action cannot refresh it, and unused bolts are lost, not banked. Outside initiative use an actually available six-second turn-equivalent Bonus Action under the existing action budget, starting hostile combat in ordinary initiative before an attack; there are no free pre-initiative bolts. Loss of legal target, consciousness, source eligibility or concentration before resolution prevents the bolt, with spent committed budgets unrefunded. Suppression prevents use but preserves original deadline and already spent bolt history; a scheduled suppressed bludgeoning pulse is lost. No continuing caster-to-center range tether, mobile sphere, independent creature/vehicle turn, automatic enemy selection or class-companion command budget is created. Concentration ending/expiry/removal ends this field and all its unused future opportunities. Each committed creation pulse finishes its snapshotted recipients before a pulse-caused concentration loss removes future effects.

CURRENT SOURCE-ORIGIN BOUNDARY: The sphere-origin attack permission is authored explicitly by this spell; it is not imported from AC6 Skybrand or a generic remote-origin feature. Owner close-quarters conditions, source-specific hit hooks and the real Bonus Action remain independent checks. The named P20-STORM-REAL-01 example uses actual Mage Undying Spellform to preserve concentration while no Bonus Action is available; no unnamed unconscious-concentration exception is granted.

## Flame Typhoon

| Class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 5 | 15 | 10 |
| Mage | Evocation | 5 | 9 | 10 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 5 | 9 | 10 |

KEEP Action, placement range 60 feet, V/S/F, concentration up to one minute, a summoned repeating arcane cannon and three rounds at 5d6 force. NEW interpret rounds as three ammunition shots total, with at most one actual shot per owner numbered round, not three shots each round for a minute. Create one magical machine object on a perceived unoccupied supporting surface within range and line of effect. Its whole Medium, five-foot occupied space must fit. NEW profile: AC 16, maximum/current HP 30, mass 100 lb, no movement of its own, poison/psychic damage immunity, no organic Wounds, Stable state or death saves. It has no creature type, independent senses, initiative, speech, hands or autonomous enemy selection. Dexterity saves fail automatically as an immobile object; for genuinely applicable STR/CON object saves use +3, and other saves require an effect that actually targets an object. It does not acquire an organic condition merely from a creature-only spell.

One mortar per caster is allowed. A genuine replacement ends the former mortar administratively, without destruction rewards; a rejected cast replaces nothing before ordinary legal cast commitment. This source has its own one-object capacity and occupies no Tinker deployment slot or Wizard creature-summon slot. It nevertheless shares the owner's existing single Bonus Action command and single damaging companion/device activation per numbered round. These limits apply across all actual owned companions and devices. A separate minion, extra Action, extra turn, reconstruction or movement cannot provide a second mortar shot that round.

NEW while conscious and able to command, the owner spends that shared Bonus Action on their own regular turn, selects one perceived creature or independently legal exposed object, and declares one shot. The mortar must be operative, have ammunition, be within 60 feet of the owner with command line of effect, and have a clear firing line to the selected target within 60 feet of the mortar. Hostile worn/carried gear is not independently targeted without an actual applicable permission. Resolve the commanded shot in the single attached companion phase after the owner's whole turn. The cast itself grants no shot, but a remaining legal Bonus Action on that same regular turn can command its first one. If the command/shot predicates fail before firing, the committed command is lost; no ammunition or damaging activation is spent when no shot begins, and no queued retargeting occurs. A later genuine command may try again while time/ammunition remain.

When the legal shot begins, spend one ammunition unit and the shared damaging activation, then make one ranged spell attack using the owner's current actual casting modifier and PB from this spell's acquired class. Hit deals 5d6 force; a natural critical doubles only those attack dice. A miss spends the shot. This is a narrow shot along its firing line, not a blast radius or arcing attack through roofs. No attack rider, subclass missile feature or creature-summon bonus applies solely because the object is a cannon; require the real source predicate. After its third resolved shot, it ends. Losing concentration, its 60-second deadline, dismissal or replacement also ends it.

The machine can be damaged or moved by a genuinely legal object-handling/forced-motion source. A moved cannon fires only while on a stable supporting surface and with legal placement; carrying it provides no mobile firing exception. Suppression makes it an inert but still physical object while its clock continues; the same HP, ammunition and spent history resume if still valid. Genuine functional-machine repair may restore positive HP under the repair source's requirements, but cannot refill shots, extend duration or reconstruct the vanished cannon. At zero HP it is destroyed and the conjured body vanishes, supplying no usable original material for Snap, harvest or salvage. Destruction and administrative ending remain distinct events for any independently qualified trigger, but Tinker-owner/class-deployment predicates are not waived. Its conjured metal/force components never become saleable equipment, permanent scrap or an ammunition supply.

Snapshot all creatures and eligible ordinary nonmagical objects/structural sections with center line of effect. Creatures, including allies and the caster when actually in the sphere, make CON: failure takes the shared 8d8 thunder; success takes half, rounded down. Each eligible object/section receives the shared 8d8 as one thunder packet against its actual material defenses and durability, without a save. No separate object-bonus die is added.

Use Shatter's reviewed object-admission and structural identity rule: unattended objects, caster-held objects or items deliberately offered by willing holders; hostile worn/carried equipment is not separately damaged. For each actual structural whole, select one contiguous exposed section wholly within the sphere, bounded by a five-foot cube, containing the reachable point nearest the center. Stable neutral geometry resolves equal choices before rolling; naming each brick or panel separately does not multiply one building's packets. An independently profiled freestanding structure or object remains genuinely distinct. Hidden boundaries are Director records, not information revealed by casting.

NEW a selected section is weak if its already established structural record marks it unstable/weak with a finite failure profile, or it is ordinary nonmagical wood, packed earth or crumbling masonry no more than one foot thick and already at or below half of its actual structural maximum HP before this blast. Missing durability is not treated as zero or automatically weak; establish the real object's finite profile before committing a spell that depends on it. Do not retrospectively invent damage or a weak support to reward a cast. Resolve thunder damage first. Any selected section actually reduced to structural zero fails normally. In addition, a prequalified weak section suffering positive post-defense thunder from this spell collapses even if its numeric HP would otherwise remain positive. Mark its structural integrity failed/zero without treating the extra loss as another damage packet or a resource-generating DamageDealt amount. Immunity preventing all its thunder means this extra collapse clause has no trigger.

After the original blast finishes, resolve real unsupported material and body falls. An existing finite structure/hazard profile controls the actual failure footprint, connected supports, height, escape and damage; a large cascade must be supported by that preexisting profile, not generated by recursively treating all newly exposed architecture as a new spell target. NEW fallback for a local weak section without a separate debris attack: its real falling material creates at most one debris event in that section's horizontal footprint plus five feet, and only up to twenty vertical feet below it. Affected creatures make DEX, taking a shared 2d6 bludgeoning on failure or half on success. Each creature receives this fallback at most once per cast, even when separate sections overlap. The same mass does not also create a second collision packet. Actual creatures losing support instead use their one ordinary Fall Marker; the same bodily fall is not counted again as the fallback debris event. No automatic burial, Prone, pinning, suffocation or arbitrary crush multiplier is supplied. A scenario requiring larger structural effects needs an explicit finite hazard before commitment; this preserves real building consequences without granting limitless spell propagation.

Rubble retains the actual source matter, provenance and damage. It is difficult terrain and offers cover only where its actual remaining geometry supplies it. It persists until real clearing, transport or repair work changes it. The spell does not rebuild a structure after expiry, conjure extra salvage, cleanse material, damage a magical force wall, deafen creatures or keep damaging later entrants.

The caster cannot move the center after creation.

The entire operative field heavily obscures normal vision for everyone, including allies and caster. A line of sight through its interior is obstructed according to the actual vision rules; this does not create physical cover or block physical line of effect, hearing or a genuinely applicable nonvisual sense. True Seeing's illusion/darkness benefits do not see through this actual magical obscuration by default, since this proposal does not define it as a false visual image or darkness. The fog does not automatically hide a known location, grant its occupants Invisible, erase attack evidence or bypass the actual perception requirement for a sight-targeted spell.

Each makes CON; failure takes the shared rolled 3d6 necrotic, success half rounded down. At later actual regular target STARTs while eligible inside, or on first actual entry in a numbered round, apply that same procedure. Across every Blood Fog application/caster, each creature receives at most one damage/save opportunity per numbered round, including creation. Mark the exposure before its save/damage, including successful saves or complete prevention. Choose the earliest operative eligible source when multiple fields qualify simultaneously. Entering/reentering, being pushed out/back, extra turns or changing controller does not clear the history. A cast cannot reveal a hidden creature's presence to its caster merely because that creature is damaged.

Enemy classification is evaluated at the actual opportunity from the caster's genuine existing knowledge, not an objective secret hostility or alignment test. A known enemy currently unseen can qualify; an entirely unknown creature is not automatically identified as one. A recipient that becomes eligible later receives no retroactive damage and needs an actual future START/entry opportunity. Area obscuration applies regardless of that damage predicate or immunity. No inhalation, blood-bearing anatomy or living-only gate is added to damage; actual necrotic defenses still determine results. Corpse/object contents are not damage recipients and provide no healing, residue or new creature event.

Outside initiative use one possible creation packet followed by six-second regular spell pulse opportunities for occupants; entering has its normal family exposure check in that shared six-second interval. On entering initiative, map the next unresolved periodic opportunity to the next actual regular START, retain the original deadline/exposure ledger, and add no transition packet. Suppressed opportunities are lost, not banked; resumption itself is neither entry nor START. Leaving the area ends exposure to obscuration/damage without ending the field. Concentration loss, actual removal or expiry ends the entire source. One Blood Fog per caster; a new genuinely formed field replaces the prior one, while ordinary concentration displacement can already have ended it. New paid casts never reset the named-family per-recipient exposure history.

If no maintenance Action is completed during an available future regular turn, the channel ends at that turn's END; a miss still counts as maintenance. Outside initiative an otherwise capable caster spends the equivalent whole Action at each successive six-second cycle; missed maintenance ends it. Loss of concentration, actual caster incapacity, target death, actual separation beyond60feet, loss of line of effect/perception, removal or expiry ends the channel immediately; suppression makes a pulse unavailable, consumes its scheduled opportunity and therefore causes missed-maintenance ending if it spans the entire relevant turn/cycle. No paused beam or stored future pulse exists. A hit's healing resolves after its damage/life consequences, even if that hit killed the target and thereby ended later pulses: caster receives ordinary base healing floor(actualDamageDealt/2), using this pulse's eligible terminal branches once and excluding duplicated parents, overkill and copied/replayed descendants. Do not halve nominal dice before counting actual damage or require ordinary HP loss when tempHP was truly absorbed. Damage transfer remains one root; sum its actual terminal recipients without healing separately for each. Apply genuine ordinary healing modifiers, broad prohibition, current maximum/Critical ceiling and source causal guards. Full casterHP, blocked healing or a miss stores nothing and refunds nothing. Unlike Lifedraw's expressly narrower profile, this proposal adds no living-nonconstruct/allied/helpless exclusion to the printed damage and heal clause; necrotic defenses still govern actual numbers. Damage/HP cannot be farmed from an invented summon resource pool or overkill. A subsequent paid cast begins a genuinely new channel and concentration, not a free same-channel extra pulse; all real Action/RP/Dissonance costs apply. The proposal's ten-pulse ceiling and continuous maintenance are NEW balance choices for joint review.

CURRENT HEALING-ELIGIBILITY COMPLETION: Exclude any terminal branch whose actual transfer/source flags forbid derived healing or recovery before summing the base-healing pool. The original NE5-06-B arithmetic is conditional on a genuinely permitted split, not a claim that Burden Shared or Life Linked allows it. The two supplied source-qualified vectors show their actual narrower results and retain the original abstract scenario unchanged. Neither vector changes the separate pending broad donor predicate for this spell.

CURRENT TARGET-CONTINUITY COMPLETION — Enervation only: "Still-existing original creature" is the target identity/lifecycle predicate, not a new living-anatomy or nonconstruct restriction. A continuing active creature classified as nonliving is not excluded solely by that classification. Its actual defenses, damage/source eligibility and no-resource/no-recovery flags still apply to each packet and any healing funding. A corpse, removed body, ended summon or destroyed entity that no longer qualifies as a creature cannot supply a maintained target. A same identity at 0 HP is tested under its actual continuing-creature/Disabled/death state, not assumed dead or alive from the HP number. The attack that actually ends the target finishes its own causal damage and source-qualified healing before future channel opportunities are canceled. This completion does not import Leeching Plague's hostile-living-nonconstruct donor filter, provide healing to a dead caster, or grant repair, resurrection, resource farming or an exception to another source's exclusion flags.

Action; range 60 feet; V/S/F. KEEP a crystal field dealing 3d6 piercing on entry or move. NEW create a fixed twenty-foot-radius footprint of conjured spines extending one foot above genuinely exposed supported surfaces, centered on a perceived point with line of effect and clipped by real barriers. Replace Instantaneous with concentration up to sixty seconds. The field does not rise through a ceiling, create support, change movement cost, restrain, block sight or become useful gems. NEW two actual triggers share one cap: a creature physically enters/contact-arrives on an active patch from outside the active field, OR actually moves at least five feet while contacting its active patches after starting inside. Entry consumes the same opportunity as movement; it is not a second packet after the first five feet. The same continuous body trajectory can qualify only once per creature per numbered round across all Crystalline Growth casts/owners. Voluntary and forced ground motion qualify; teleport arrival onto an active patch qualifies as entry, but teleporting across without contact does not. Airborne passage wholly above the one-foot spines does not qualify. Creation under a stationary creature is neither entry nor movement; standing still supplies no periodic tick. In-place gestures, attacks and another creature’s turn supply no movement trigger. At a genuine eligible exposure make DEX: failure takes 3d6 magical piercing, success half. A simultaneous entry group shares one raw roll; distinct actual exposures roll separately. Ordinary defenses and exact hazard permissions apply; shoes alone supply no generic hazard immunity. Formation/suppression lifting creates no fictitious entry. NEW physical removal profile: partition the footprint into fixed scene-aligned five-foot patches, clipping edge patches to the true radius; each patch has AC 13 and HP 10, with no additional resistance/immunity beyond actual object rules. A legal reachable object attack can damage one actual patch, with no invented multi-patch attack or harm to creatures standing there. At zero HP that patch is permanently cleared for this instance; there is no regrowth, separate cheap spike target or second salvage claim. Effects including objects can affect patches only within their real geometry. Suppression makes affected spines inactive and nonobstructive while preserving damage/cleared-state history and deadline; resumption restores only uncleared patches with no free damage trigger. Ending the source removes all remaining spines and leaves actual unrelated damage/cargo unchanged. No harvestable crystal, field relocation or initial damage is added.

CURRENT CONTACT-DISTANCE COMPLETION — NEW: Accumulate actual distance traveled while the body contacts an active patch during the current regular movement opportunity, including qualified forced motion. Pauses, frame steps and changes of input command do not reset that accumulator. A movement exposure occurs at five total contacted feet after starting inside, not five feet of nominal Speed expenditure; terrain affects paid budget separately. Genuine entry from outside can trigger earlier and then spends the same family cap. Clearing a patch removes its contact volume, HP and damage opportunity for this instance. Each fixed scene-aligned patch is one object identity regardless of how many visual spikes it renders.

KEEP6d6force and the choice of slow OR Prone in an area. NEW select one perceived point within60feet/line of effect and a20-foot-radius sphere centered there. All actual creatures in the sphere with origin-to-body line of effect are recipients,including allies/caster;no selective enemy filter or arbitrary object durability damage is added. Before commitment choose one rider for the entire cast:Slow or Prone. Each creature makes DEX against spellDC. Roll6d6once for the shared packet;success takes half,rounded down,and no rider. Failure takes full damage and independently receives the chosen rider if still a legal active creature and not protected against that component. Damage immunity alone does not cancel an otherwise admitted rider.

Slow means half of each actually available locomotion Speed through the target's first future regular END,excluding any in-progress target turn,or6elapsed seconds outside initiative. It is one ordinary adverse-Speed record using actual strongest-control comparison before real later systemic multipliers. It grants no AC penalty,attack limit,Action denial,Reaction restriction,spell delay or teleport anchoring. Do not import either full ordinary Slow school mode merely from the word slow. The rider is nonconcentration and cannot acquire a fresh endpoint through suppression or extra turns. Recasting does not combine multiple halves into one-quarter Speed;real independent records retain their own clocks.

Prone applies the actual native condition immediately and remains until the creature genuinely stands or another real rule ends it. It has no fabricated one-round automatic cure or extra duration. A swimming/flying/hovering subject follows the real condition and locomotion rules;the spatial appearance alone does not drag it toward the ground or create an extra fall beyond actual loss of support. A creature already Prone receives no additional Prone penalty. No compulsory standing Action is invented;normal movement/ability restrictions govern standing.

Neither rider spends whole-Action hard-control admission. Actual immunities/protection can prevent the covered component without preventing the independently eligible force packet. Ordinary attack/save timing,damage transfer,temporaryHP,Wounds,InstantDeath and concentration checks resolve once with actual ownership. A transfer of part of the damage does not transfer the original target's Slow/Prone rider absent a real source saying so. A creature that dies from the packet receives no new living movement condition;an actual survival replacement is respected before deciding that eligibility.

This spell creates no portal,rift entity,other plane,teleport,pull/push,terrain alteration,ongoing force field or repeated damage. The area is a single snapshot at resolution and never expands as another effect ends. After damage,only any actual Slow rider or Prone condition remains under its own rules;Prone is not an ongoing magical hold that ordinary dispel removes merely because a spell caused it. Eligible removal of the Slow application can end that rider but cannot reverse the packet. All original source metadata remains preserved.

Action; casting-point range20feet; V/S/F. KEEP both source placements and8d6 psychic. NEW school-bound modes preserve rather than flatten their differences: the actual Enchantment class-access row creates a twenty-foot-diameter sphere (radius10), and penalizes attacks and ability checks; the actual Evocation row creates the explicitly stated twenty-foot-radius sphere and penalizes d20 attacks, ability checks and saving throws. Choose a perceived center within20feet with caster line of effect. Affect every creature in the applicable sphere with center line of effect, including caster/allies when geometrically present; no INT threshold, language or mind-reading qualification is added. Each creature makes one INT save. NEW success halves psychic damage and prevents this cast’s penalty; failure takes full psychic damage and independently attempts the source-owned penalty. Psychic defenses apply only to damage; a mind-effect immunity prevents the penalty only if its actual text covers this effect, not merely because its title sounds mental. Damage resolves before attachment; a dead recipient receives no rider. NEW penalty lifetime: no concentration, ending at that recipient’s third future original regular-turn END after application (outside initiative18seconds). At each of those ENDs the recipient may make an INT save to end this rider early; the current penalty applies to that save only in the Evocation mode. At the third marker expire before any redundant repeat save. No damage repeats. Enchantment never penalizes saves. Advantage/disadvantage selects the d20 normally; replacing that d20 within the same resolution keeps the same penalty die. A genuinely new roll resolution gets a new penalty die. NEW only one Synaptic Static penalty die applies to a given roll across casters/modes; select the earliest established eligible unsuppressed rider for that roll, with stable cast-ID tie-breaking. Other riders retain their own original clocks/repeat saves and can become relevant after that one ends. An actual Bane penalty can coexist because it is a different source effect; apply each once only in its own categories. A later Synaptic Static cast resolves its own damage and may establish its own finite rider, but does not reset older endpoints. Actual suppression suspends only eligible components and keeps the endpoints; no missed save or penalty is replayed. This soft roll penalty consumes no whole-Action hard-control allowance and supplies no Charmed, Frightened, confusion, incapacitation or knowledge of thoughts.

CURRENT RNG BOUNDARY: The penalty component is private unless a separately authorized original-roll presentation includes that component. A displayed original d20 event does not automatically expose every modifier cause or grant Fate Coin permission to alter a secret penalty die. Retain each penalty die under the same original resolution through any independently permitted d20 replacement. Exact DE4 final07 now supplies its accepted lineage, component and five-entry presentation boundaries. This source adds no presentation entry. An otherwise eligible OVERT-ATTACK original d20 set may be disclosed under its own predicates, while this private penalty die and hidden modifier causes remain unavailable. A d20 replacement keeps this same original penalty component; it cannot use a new lineage or extra die draw to reroll that penalty. No new reroll permission or universal visibility is granted.

KEEP Action, 60 feet, V/S/F, one target, 4d6 psychic per round and WIS ending saves. Preserve Core Instantaneous as a metadata conflict. NEW one perceived creature with line of effect makes initial WIS; success establishes nothing and deals no damage. Failure establishes a private mental nightmare with an independently real magical psychic-damage component, concentration up to 60 elapsed seconds. Ordinary blindness/deafness is not immunity to a mental nightmare. No Frightened, Stunned, paralysis, compulsory movement or Action loss is imported from either lower Phantasmal Killer placement; no hard-control claim is made for damage alone.

On failed initial admission, deal 4d6 psychic immediately only if this named spell family's exposure for that target in the actual numbered round is unused. At each subsequent actual regular target START after mandatory start processing, an operative application attempts the same packet. Across every Greater Phantasmal Killer caster/application on that recipient, allow at most one packet per numbered round, including initial packets; select the earliest active eligible application, with neutral source/application order for ties. Record the opportunity before resolving its damage, so immunity or an HP floor does not permit another source to retry that exposure. A wholly suppressed application cannot supply a packet; its scheduled opportunity is lost, not banked. Recasting does not clear that target's family history. The lower Phantasmal Killer is a different named source with its own preserved 4d10 clauses and caps; no silent numerical upgrade, downgrade or cross-family merger is proposed.

The target repeats WIS at every affected actual regular END, including the upcoming END of a turn already underway. Success ends only the corresponding application before any later packet, with no additional damage attached to that successful save. Separate applications have separate ending saves and concentration, even though their damage shares the family exposure cap. Outside initiative there is the eligible immediate application packet, then future START-type packet opportunities every six seconds after application and END-only saves on the same six-second boundaries, resolving packet before ending save when both are due. On entering initiative map the first unresolved packet/save to actual next regular START/END without an extra transition packet, preserving original expiry and spent family exposure. At the exact maximum deadline expiry wins.

True Seeing or recognizing the nightmare identifies its false depiction but, by this explicit NEW separation, does not itself remove the independently sustained damaging spell. Actual protection against this mental illusion can prevent/suppress the whole application where its text says so; psychic immunity prevents only damage, while Fear immunity alone has no effect here. No mental image supplies the caster private memories, remote senses or the target's hidden location. The spell has no continuing range tether after establishment. Target death, actual removal, concentration ending or expiry ends its application. Suppression does not stop its clock or scheduled ending saves; no extra tick occurs on resumption. These choices preserve the lower printed 4d6 amount and deliberately place its comparison with lower-rank 4d10 in the joint issue register.

### reason

Four future END installments, no immediate fifth packet or generic disease, are explicit source completions. Separately concur with this spell's proposed hostile-living-nonconstruct donor and continuing-living-caster healing constraints; they are not universal life/necromancy rules. First remove no-derived-healing/no-recovery branches, then apply the donor filter, sum terminal tempHP+HP losses once, and floor half once. No overkill, duplicate parent, corpse, controlled resource donor or dead-caster healing is invented.

No Leeching donor filter is silently imported.

Precommit one failed-save rider for the cast: half current Speeds for one future END or ordinary Prone. This is not full Slow, a portal, a new plane or transferred control. Damage immunity does not automatically prevent a still-qualified independent rider; actual post-damage recipient lifecycle must still permit it. Hover/Falling and transferred-hit identity retain their actual source rules.
